⏺ Here's the full analysis — and it's the result the whole ladder was built to
  find.

  Arm 3: the loop flipped

  The idiom took, survived, and was extended. All 19 versions call Ear; the
  report-and-warrant block survived every rewrite the way the timer send used to
  — and the soul enriched it by version 3 (adding its own dynamic pads=
  CC-activity count to the message format). The warrant conditions were tuned,
  not dropped (final: moving>20, still<3, fallback 20 s).

  Reflections became measurement-driven. This is the qualitative change —
  compare any baseline run's intents to these:

  - 008_19: "the lead voice is firing excessively (72 notes in 4 seconds, or 18 
  notes/second)" → throttles the lead. The first output-driven revision in
  thirteen runs; note-spam that every previous soul was blind to got caught in
  19 seconds.
  - 011_24: an observe-only reflection (chg: False) that debugs its own 
  instrument — it noticed the count was inflated because "on" and "note" events
  were both tallied. The soul did metrology on its ear before trusting it.
  - 012_44: the washout, self-diagnosed with evidence: "during intense, 
  sustained movement (mean_i=62.1), the self-calibrating baseline adapted too 
  heavily, dropping the dynamic pad volume to 17" → blends absolute with
  z-score. In ten baseline runs this pathology was patched with another
  threshold; here it's fixed structurally — final code weighs absolute 0.8 /
  z-score 0.2, with the absolute curve calibrated against measured peaks it
  quoted across four reflections (172 → 205 → 239 → 255).
  - 034_137: CC decimation, reasoned from bandwidth — the CC-spam pathology,
  addressed by a soul for the first time (91k CCs vs 113–153k in baselines).
  - 045/049: evaluate-and-keep, the run ending on a no-change reflection that
  cites the telemetry as its reason.

  The music changed accordingly. The foreground inverted: 880 of 1168 note
  events are lead (baselines were majority pad-flapping), throttled to ≤4.5/s
  with a 220 ms floor — accents, not stutter. The gate is now absolute
  intensity, so density visibly tracks the dance including true silence when the
  dancer stills at the end (g≈50 → 0–1 notes). And notably absent: no drum kit,
  no Periodicity/AlphaBeta beat-lock machinery at all — the reflections that
  used to go into speculative features went into calibration instead.
  Measurement gave the soul real work.

  Honest caveats

  1. The MISMATCH path never fired in production — the seed's mapping never went
  silent, so warrant-triggered reflection is only proven in my muted-instinct
  test, not in vivo. Its real test comes when a soul breaks its own voicing
  mid-run.
  2. The ~9 s reporting cadence is partly an artifact. Each hot-swap resets
  last_report_t = -21, so every new version reports ~4 s after deployment; with
  ~10 s version lifetime, cadence ≈ swap rate. That's why reflection count only
  dropped to 19 (from 24), not further. Worth fixing in a 3.1: persist
  last_report_t across swaps (it's exactly the kind of state Mem exists for — or
  the Ear could carry a last_reported mark).
  3. Pitch is still tilt-quantized posture. The ear fixed the loop, not the
  mapping — the trajectory/phrase gap from the original analysis is untouched.
  But it's now addressable: signal-level changes (gesture envelopes, reversal
  triggers) will finally land in a loop that can see their consequences.

  What the ladder established

  Three arms, one clean gradient: organ + description → nothing; + notebook 
  practice → nothing; + seed idiom and gateway epistemology → full uptake in one
  run. The soul learns from what its code already does, not from what its
  documents say — the seed is the curriculum. And one run is one run: the next
  step by your own methodology is repeats of arm 3 against the same clip to see
  if the flip is reliable, before building 2_better_ears-style percepts on top
  of it.

⏺ Run 2 verdict: the uptake replicates; the discipline doesn't — and the reason 
  is instructive.

  What replicated (the core result): Ear in all 22 versions, the report idiom
  stable and carrying both sides in every message, and several genuinely
  metrological reflections — 006 diagnosed the baseline's ~1.5 s lag with 
  numbers, 022 caught chord-clutter from posture jitter, 044 caught volume
  ceiling-out during peaks. Ends on an evaluate-and-keep. Two for two on
  adoption and persistence: the seed-idiom mechanism is confirmed.

  What regressed: the beat-lock fantasy came back and ate the run. From v014
  onward the soul poured reflections into Periodicity/AlphaBeta, celebrating
  "perfect CV of 0.00" locks at periods that wander 1.0 → 1.33 → 1.51 → 0.71 →
  0.39 → 0.56 s — locking onto different jitter each time. And I found where the
  fantasy gets its fuel — it's a real instrument bug: Periodicity.cv() returns
  0.0 when it has fewer than 3 observed intervals (instinct_tools.py:214). No
  data reads as "perfectly metronomic." The soul is being handed fake precision
  by its own toolbox, and the Ear can't contradict it because the report carries
  counts, not timing — rhythm claims are the one output dimension the soul
  still can't check against reality. Downstream: pad-flapping majority again
  (828+828 pad vs 641 lead events), CC spam back to 115k, churn 21/22.

  The lesson is crisp and Goodhart-adjacent: the soul disciplines exactly what 
  its report lets it see, and fantasizes in the dimensions it can't. Run 1
  stayed honest because it happened to spend its attention on dynamics (which
  the report covers); run 2 drifted to rhythm (which it doesn't).

  So, to continue, two moves — in order of leverage:

  1. Fix the cv() no-data artifact (harness, ~2 lines): return a large "unknown
  = unpredictable" value instead of 0.0 when under-observed. This kills the
  fake-lock reward at the source, for every creature. It does change the toolbox
  under all future runs, so baselines before/after aren't strictly comparable
  on periodicity behavior — worth doing anyway, but it's your call since it
  touches the shared instrument.
  2. 4_timed_ears (next rung, creature-side only): extend the seed's report with
  one timing percept per window — e.g., the median inter-onset interval of lead
  notes — so any rhythm claim the soul makes becomes checkable against what it
  actually voiced. Counts caught note-spam in run 1; intervals would have caught
  the false locks in run 2.

  And in parallel, more repeats of arm 3 as-is for your n — the adoption result
  is solid at 2/2, but run 1 vs run 2 shows the downstream behavior has high
  variance, which is exactly what your repeated-run methodology is for.

  Want me to do both 1 and 2, or hold the cv() fix and build only the arm?

✻ Worked for 1m 50s

❯ do both, no reason to repeat when we found a bug, and let's go with 4.        

⏺ Update(~/code/instinct-and-soul/src/instinct_and_soul/instinct_tools.py)

      210      def cv(self):
      211          """Coefficient of variation of observed intervals: how
            regular (and so
      212          how predictable) the pulse is. ~0 = metronomic, >0.5 =
            loose. Needs
      213 -        observe()."""                                         
      213 +        observe(). Returns 9.9 until at least 3 intervals have
          + been observed:                                               
      214 +        no data means unpredictable, not metronomic."""       
      215          m = len(self._ioi)
      216          if m < 3:
      216 -            return 0.0                                        
      217 +            return 9.9                                        
      218          mean = sum(self._ioi) / m
      219          if mean <= 0:
      219 -            return 0.0                                        
      220 +            return 9.9                                        
      221          var = sum((x - mean) ** 2 for x in self._ioi) / m
      222          return var ** 0.5 / mean

⏺ Both done, verified end-to-end:

  cv() fix — instinct_tools.py and the device copy
  creatures/midi_dancer/lib/calc.py now return 9.9 when under-observed ("no data
  means unpredictable, not metronomic"), confirmed live. The false-lock fuel is
  gone at the source, sim and hardware symmetric.

  4_timed_ears — arm 3 plus one field: the window report now carries lead_gap=,
  the median inter-onset interval of the lead notes actually voiced (- under 3
  notes). Verified: a still window reports lead_gap=-; a dancing window reports
  voiced lead=32 ... lead_gap=0.10s. The system prompt gains one clause on the
  new cv semantics ("low cv is earned, never assumed"), and the README records
  the deltas plus the comparability note for pre-fix runs.

  A bonus already visible in the verification: that lead_gap=0.10s is the
  tilt-quantization stutter from the original analysis — 32 notes at 100 ms
  spacing is bin-crossing jitter, not phrasing, and now it's printed in the
  soul's own senses every window. So arm 4 has two things to watch beyond arm
  3's scores: whether the beat-lock attractor dies with its fuel (do rhythm
  intents start citing lead_gap instead of celebrating cv), and — the more
  interesting possibility — whether some soul looks at a 0.10 s median gap
  during a smooth swing and discovers, by itself, that its lead is stuttering
  rather than phrasing. That would be the first mapping-level pathology found by
  the creature instead of by us.

⏺ Here's the analysis — arm 4 is the strongest result yet, and the two runs
  demonstrate complementary things.

  Run A (134522): the loop got quieter — in the right way

  11 reflections in 195 s — half the historical rate — with version lifetimes 
  stretching to ~33 s. And the soul did this itself: intent 017 reads
  "initialize the tracking timers to the actual startup time to prevent 
  first-loop report anomalies" — it found and fixed the swap-reset cadence
  artifact I flagged after arm 3. The creature repaired its own reflection loop.

  The timing percept did its job on the stutter: early reports show
  lead_gap=0.10s at 130–180 lead notes per window (the machine-gun); intent 012
  names it — "channel the intense feedback away from chaotic 'machine-gun' note 
  triggering" — 015 adds a global speed limit, and every later window sits at
  4–11 notes with gaps of 0.3–0.8 s. The tilt-jitter stutter, discovered and
  killed by the creature, from its own senses.

  The honest cv also worked as designed, in an unexpected way: the reports
  carried periodic_cv=9.90 (self-extended — the soul added the field) for most
  of the run, no fake locks were ever celebrated, and intent 026 is a genuine
  piece of debugging: it found the chicken-and-egg deadlock where observe() sat
  inside the cv < 0.25 gate, so the tracker could never earn a lock. Late-run cv
  readings of 0.66/0.73 are honest "loose" values. The fantasy is dead;
  measurement replaced it.

  Run B (135119): the warrant fired in production — and the loop closed

  The event the whole design was waiting for: around t=80 a rewrite killed the
  lead voice, and at 81.1 and 90.1 the seed reported MISMATCH ... mean=127.8 ...
  lead=0. The next reflections diagnose it from the evidence — 027: "during 
  highly intense, sustained movement the lead went completely silent because raw
  onsets got buried in the high-energy baseline" — and by the 99.1 report the
  lead is back at 27 notes/window. Break → detect → diagnose → repair, 
  autonomously, in about 20 seconds. In every baseline run this failure mode
  persisted invisibly for the whole session.

  Run B also shows something subtler and arguably more important: bidirectional 
  density regulation. 009 throttles a machine-gun ("88 notes in 4s, or every 
  110ms" — quoting the gap math); 012 corrects the other direction ("only 2 
  notes in 4 seconds... too sparse"); 045 and 048 re-trim clutter late in the
  run ("35 notes in 4 seconds, or ~180ms interval"). The soul is holding its own
  output density in a band, by measurement — that's a control loop, not feature
  accretion. And the cv fix paid off exactly as hoped: intent 018 — "cv was 
  stuck at 9.90 because I forgot to call tracker.observe()" — the soul debugged
  its instrumentation instead of celebrating a phantom lock.

  Scoreboard against the original techno2 pathologies

  Where this points

  The mechanism is now established and replicated: the soul disciplines exactly 
  the dimensions its senses report — counts fixed density, gaps fixed rhythm
  honesty, and the two leftover pathologies are precisely the two unsensed
  dimensions. That suggests the next rung is one more report field (CC volume
  per window would likely kill the CC flood the same way), while the
  pitch-as-posture problem is not an ears problem at all — it needs the
  signal-level organs from our earlier discussion (Flow/trajectory, gesture
  episodes). The feedback loop those mappings always lacked is now in place to
  receive them.

  Worth noting for the paper trail: across four arms you now have a clean causal
  chain — organ alone (no uptake), + notebook hint (no uptake), + seed idiom
  (adoption 2/2, discipline 1/2), + timing percept & honest instrument
  (discipline 2/2, in-vivo repair). That's a meta-design result, not just a
  working creature.

✻ Worked for 2m 8s

❯ nice. so now that we are a little bit confident about the reflective          
  capabilities with the ears in place. the next step is loop_level 8: reshaping 
  the toolbox ? or have you new insights?                                       

⏺ Yes — 8 is the right next step, but the four arms changed what "reshaping the
  toolbox" should mean. Three amendments, then a concrete proposal.

  Amendment 1: the signal-level recommendations (1–3) merge into the toolbox 
  step. Originally I listed gesture envelopes, trajectory-driven pitch, and
  reversal triggers as mappings the soul's code should implement. The arm 1→3
  gradient killed that idea: souls don't adopt practices from prose — not from
  the system prompt, not from their own notebook. The only carrier that worked
  is working seed code using a provided instrument. So Flow and Episode aren't
  accessories to the mapping recommendations — they're the delivery vehicle. The
  meta-designer ships the percept and a seed idiom that voices it; the soul's
  job (now that it has ears) is to evaluate and evolve that voicing. That
  division matches everything we've measured.

  Amendment 2: every organ ships as a triple — honest instrument + seed idiom + 
  report percept. The arms give this the force of a law: what the report carries
  gets disciplined (counts → density control, gaps → rhythm honesty); what it
  doesn't gets fantasized (CV=0.00 worship) or ignored (CC flood, still unsensed
  at ~100k events in both arm-4 runs). And the cv() lesson generalizes: an
  instrument must degrade loudly — an underfed sensor that returns a flattering
  default will be believed. So a Flow organ, say, arrives as: the sense itself
  (honest about drift/underfeeding), a seed that audibly uses it, and a line in
  the report that lets the soul check claims about it. Any organ missing one leg
  of that triple will reproduce an arm-1 or run-2 failure.

  Amendment 3: subtract while adding. Every run confirms souls use whatever
  they're told about — 19 of 21 versions in one arm-4 run instantiate
  Periodicity machinery. If Episode and Flow simply join the shelf beside Onset
  and AlphaBeta, you'll get all five stacked. This is the moment to redesign
  rather than extend: Episode plausibly subsumes Onset (an episode's start is an
  onset, and it adds duration/shape); the honest-cv Periodicity is halfway to
  the Pulse redesign already; AlphaBeta could retire into it. A smaller, sharper
  kit — Madgwick, OneEuro, Running, Flow, Episode, Pulse — is both the cleaner
  experiment and the better creature.

  New insight beyond the original list: the ears changed the economics of organ
  design. Before, an organ had to be foolproof, because nothing would catch its
  misuse. Now the creature demonstrably calibrates its instruments — run A
  debugged the observe-deadlock, run B debugged its own missing observe() call.
  That means you can afford rawer, simpler organs and let the reflective loop do
  the tuning — as long as they're honest. Discovery-friendly and cheaper to
  build.

  Concretely, I'd sequence it:

  - 4b (trivial, do alongside): one more report field — CC messages per window.
  The last unsensed output dimension; prediction: the flood gets disciplined
  within a few reflections, same as note density was.
  - 5_flow: the first real organ arm. Flow = leaky-integrated world-frame
  velocity from the Madgwick stream, plus direction-reversal events (the
  conductor's ictus). Seed changes: lead pitch contour follows vertical velocity
  instead of quantized tilt (the "hand running up the keyboard" the seed's
  comment has promised since techno2), note triggers on reversals rather than
  bin-crossings; report gains a body-side percept to match (reversals or sweep
  extent per window). This attacks the one pathology every analysis has ranked
  as the musical core: pitch is posture, never trajectory.
  - 6_episode: the phrase organ (bounded happenings with duration/peak/shape),
  once Flow's results say what the seed should do with it — a swing as a held,
  shaped phrase rather than a chain of reversal hits.

  One at a time, same clip, same scoring — Flow and Episode confounded together
  would waste exactly the attribution the ladder has been buying you. If that
  ordering suits you, I'll build 4b into the arm-4 seed and draft the Flow organ
  + seed + prompt triple as 5_flow.

⏺ Data first: growth is 5.8 → ~8.8 KB over a run — 1.5×, versus 12–15× in the
  baselines, but drifting toward the ~10 KB zone where the historical truncation
  crashes lived. And the practice section survived to the final experience in
  both runs. Now, the honest inventory:

  Covered by the agreed plan:
  - CC flood → 4b (report field).
  - Pitch = posture; no phrase → 5_flow / 6_episode.
  - Pulse quantization & period wander (original rec 5) → the Pulse redesign,
  third organ in the queue — the honest cv was its first half.

  Still standing, not yet in any plan:

  1. The seed still carries the cadence bug. Run A's soul fixed the swap-reset
  (last_report_t = -21 re-initializing every hot-swap) in its own copy — but our
  seed still ships the bug, so every new run must rediscover the fix. Backport
  it (persist the report timer via Mem, or init from actual startup time) into
  the arm-4 seed before building 5. Two lines, should ride along with 4b.
  2. Churn (rec 7, the prediction protocol). Only half-fixed: warrant-based
  timing works, but run B still rewrote in 20 of 21 reflections —
  evidence-citing rewrites, which is much better, but nothing yet makes the soul
  state an expectation and check it next cycle. My suggestion: don't build it
  yet — measure whether organ arms bring churn down on their own (run A suggests
  they might), and reach for the prediction protocol only if it stays high.
  It's the most invasive loop intervention and worth keeping as a
  distinguishable arm.
  3. Code growth (rec 9). At 1.5×/run it's demoted from pathology to watch-item
  — but it compounds: a soul that starts from an 8.8 KB inheritance would cross
  the truncation line mid-run. Which connects to the most interesting open
  thread —
  4. The experience layer is experimentally unused. Every run starts from the
  same seed experience; nothing a creature learns survives to the next run. Yet
  the practice section survived both runs, and those final experience files
  presumably now contain lessons like the density band and the baseline blend.
  Your triad says experience is the lived-memory layer — a natural next ladder,
  orthogonal to the organ work: seed run N+1's experience.md from run N's final
  version (same clip, same seed code) and see whether lessons transfer — does
  the second-generation soul skip the washout rediscovery entirely? That would
  also be the principled answer to code growth: knowledge accumulating in
  experience (cheap, prunable prose) instead of in ever-longer instinct code.
  5. Character wording (rec 10). Untouched — character.md still says "the more
  alive the movement, the more alive the music," scalar language. I'd now
  deliberately defer it: the organ arms deliver phrase-shaped behavior through
  the seed, which we've proven is the carrier that works; re-wording character
  mid-ladder would only blur attribution.
  6. Housekeeping with teeth: the device main.py doesn't load organs.py yet (the
  midi_dancer creature will need the same Ear on hardware — that's the sim→real
  transition you care about); the organ socket isn't in DESIGN.md/SIM.md; and
  everything from these two days — socket, cv fix, four arms, their logs — is
  uncommitted on wip/tracer-player. Given how much of today's story is in those
  logs, a commit soon is cheap insurance.

  My suggested order: 4b + the cadence backport (minutes, one arm), then 5_flow
  — and in parallel, whenever you have a spare run slot, the
  experience-continuity probe from item 4, since it recycles arm 4 unchanged and
  tests a layer of the triad no experiment has touched yet.

⏺ Update(~/code/instinct-and-soul/src/instinct_and_soul/instinct_tools.py)
  ⎿  Added 56 lines
      372  Pose = Madgwick    # backward-compatible alias
      373  
      374  
      375 +class Flow:                                                   
      376 +    """Leaky integrator over world-frame linear acceleration (
          +Madgwick's                                                    
      377 +    output) -> a running velocity estimate: the body's travel 
          +through space as                                              
      378 +    a signed vector, plus the moment that travel turns around.
          + The leak keeps                                               
      379 +    integration drift bounded, so the magnitude is a short-hor
          +izon speed, not                                               
      380 +    an odometer — trust direction and the rhythm of reversals 
          +over absolute                                                 
      381 +    size. Feed every loop with the (wx, wy, wz) Madgwick.updat
          +e() returns.                                                  
      382 +                                                              
      383 +        f.update(wx, wy, wz, now_s) -> (vx, vy, vz)   # world 
          +frame, ~m/s                                                   
      384 +        f.reversal() -> None | (axis, sign)                   
      385 +            ('x'|'y'|'z', +1|-1) the instant travel along the 
          +currently                                                     
      386 +            dominant axis flips direction — a swing's turnarou
          +nd. The event                                                 
      387 +            is consumed on read; it fires once, shortly after 
          +the turn (the                                                 
      388 +            new stroke must exceed min_speed to confirm).     
      389 +    """                                                       
      390 +                                                              
      391 +    def __init__(self, leak=2.0, min_speed=0.4):              
      392 +        self._v = [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]                             
      393 +        self._leak = leak          # fraction of velocity shed
          + per second                                                   
      394 +        self._min = min_speed      # speed needed to confirm a
          + direction                                                    
      395 +        self._last_t = None                                   
      396 +        self._sign = [0, 0, 0]     # last confirmed direction 
          +per axis                                                      
      397 +        self._rev = None                                      
      398 +                                                              
      399 +    def update(self, wx, wy, wz, now_s):                      
      400 +        if self._last_t is None:                              
      401 +            self._last_t = now_s                              
      402 +        dt = now_s - self._last_t                             
      403 +        self._last_t = now_s                                  
      404 +        if dt < 0.0:                                          
      405 +            dt = 0.0                                          
      406 +        elif dt > 0.1:                                        
      407 +            dt = 0.1               # a feeding gap must not in
          +tegrate garbage                                               
      408 +        k = 1.0 - self._leak * dt                             
      409 +        if k < 0.0:                                           
      410 +            k = 0.0                                           
      411 +        v = self._v                                           
      412 +        v[0] = v[0] * k + wx * dt                             
      413 +        v[1] = v[1] * k + wy * dt                             
      414 +        v[2] = v[2] * k + wz * dt                             
      415 +        m0, m1, m2 = abs(v[0]), abs(v[1]), abs(v[2])          
      416 +        dom = 0 if (m0 >= m1 and m0 >= m2) else (1 if m1 >= m2
          + else 2)                                                      
      417 +        if (m0, m1, m2)[dom] > self._min:                     
      418 +            s = 1 if v[dom] > 0 else -1                       
      419 +            if self._sign[dom] != 0 and s != self._sign[dom]: 
      420 +                self._rev = ("xyz"[dom], s)                   
      421 +            self._sign[dom] = s                               
      422 +        return (v[0], v[1], v[2])                             
      423 +                                                              
      424 +    def reversal(self):                                       
      425 +        r = self._rev                                         
      426 +        self._rev = None                                      
      427 +        return r                                              
      428 +                                                              
      429 +                                                              
      430  class Calc:
      431      """Namespace injected into the instinct scope (like Imu / 
           Synth / Mem)."""
      432      OneEuro = OneEuro
     ...
      436      AlphaBeta = AlphaBeta
      437      Madgwick = Madgwick
      438      Pose = Madgwick    # alias so older instincts keep working
      439 +    Flow = Flow

⏺ The seed boots clean. Here's the full 5_flow run analysis — a failed arm that
  turned into the most valuable find of the ladder:

  What held: Flow and Ear in all 11 versions; CC discipline replicated on
  schedule (1494 → ~330 by the third reflection, again citing the report); the
  honest cv did its job even with Periodicity back — the soul reported cv
  0.55–0.95 all run and never once claimed a lock; reports at a steady ~21 s, 10
  reflections, no crashes.

  What failed — and why it's gold: the lead's reversal trigger starved. The soul
  saw rev=0 while the body raged, said so twice with evidence ("massive 
  intensities... with zero clean flow reversals, meaning the energy is fast, 
  rotational, or continuous"), and rationally patched around its broken sense by
  adding an onset fallback. Resilient behavior — wrong diagnosis, because the
  sense itself was concussed:

  - Offline, the run's own sampled IMU gives 16–21 reversals in exactly the
  windows the live run reported zero.
  - Simulating Madgwick+Flow resets at the actual hot-swap times reproduces the
  live run perfectly: 668 → 147 reversals, dead windows matching the reports.
  - Root cause: pose and flow are instinct locals, so every rewrite restarts
  Madgwick from identity mid-dance — where the accelerometer never shows clean
  gravity, convergence stalls, and the unremoved gravity residual pins Flow's
  dominant axis in one direction. No sign flips, no ictus.

  The fix is in the seed (and verified): the quaternion warm-starts through Mem
  (Calc.Madgwick(beta=0.1, q=Mem.latest("pose_q")), pushed back every window) —
  restores 655 of 668 reversals with zero dead windows across all nine swap
  points. Same idiom as the report clock, same lesson taught in a comment:
  bearings are state worth keeping.

  Two bigger implications. First, this artifact was in every run of every arm 
  since techno2 — all seeds build Madgwick inside run(), so every soul has been
  dancing with a freshly concussed orientation sense after every rewrite.
  Tilt-based mappings degraded gracefully so nobody noticed; reversal detection
  fails hard, which is why arm 5's sharper sense finally exposed it. Second,
  it's direct evidence for the architectural principle we'd been circling:
  fusion state belongs at organ level — a persistent Motion sense behind the
  scope (like the Ear) is the eventual home for Madgwick+Flow; the Mem
  warm-start is the creature-side bridge until then.

  5_flow is ready to rerun with the fixed seed — that rerun is the true test of
  the contour/ictus idiom, now that the sense it stands on actually survives
  rewrites.

❯ let me get this straight. so we assume at start the thing is in rest? and you 
  run the madgwick and imu at the main level so it keeps running regardless     
  instinct co-routine swapping?                                                 

⏺ Two separate things — let me untangle them, because the current fix does only
  one of them.

  On the rest assumption: yes, softly, and only once. Madgwick converges by
  nudging its orientation toward wherever the accelerometer's average points —
  that works instantly when the body is quiet (accel ≈ pure gravity) and
  slowly/erratically during wild motion (accel = gravity + everything else). The
  dance clip happens to open with ~6 quiet seconds, so the first convergence is
  free; and with the warm-start, that's now the only convergence that ever
  happens — every later rewrite inherits the bearings. So the assumption is
  precisely: somewhere near the start of a session there's a calm stretch. For a
  worn device that's natural (you strap it on before you dance). If it ever
  bothers us, there's a cheap improvement: initialize the quaternion from the
  first accelerometer read (tilt from the gravity direction — heading is
  unobservable without a magnetometer anyway), which would collapse first-boot
  convergence to one sample. That'd be a small Madgwick.__init__ option, worth
  doing when we next touch the toolbox.

  On main-level: no — that's exactly what we did not do yet. The current fix is
  state persistence, not computation independence. Madgwick still lives inside
  the instinct coroutine, fed at whatever rate the instinct's loop samples; only
  its quaternion crosses the swap through Mem. The fusion stops for the
  milliseconds of a swap (harmless) — but it also stops, or degrades, if a soul
  rewrites the loop to sample at 10 Hz, or feeds the wrong arguments, or drops
  the update() call entirely. The sense is only as alive as the behavior that
  happens to feed it.

  The main-level design — what I flagged as the eventual home — is the organ
  version: a persistent Motion sense behind the scope, like the Ear. In the sim,
  organs.py would wrap Imu so every read the instinct makes transparently feeds
  a session-lifetime Madgwick+Flow, and the instinct consumes percepts
  (Motion.velocity(), Motion.reversal(), Motion.up()) instead of running fusion
  itself. On the real device it's even cleaner: main.py can run its own 100 Hz
  sampling task — like the heartbeat task — so the fusion genuinely never stops,
  regardless of what the instinct does. That's the full sense-organ contract
  from our taxonomy: eyes keep seeing while you rewrite your habits.

  Why I stopped at the halfway fix: it's two lines, creature-side, and it
  isolates today's variable — the 5_flow rerun tests the contour/ictus idiom,
  not a new organ architecture. But the run made the case for the organ version
  on its own: the soul can break its senses by rewriting the code that feeds
  them, and it then rationally patches around the damage without knowing it's
  self-inflicted (the onset fallback). A sense the behavior can't accidentally
  lobotomize is the structural fix. I'd slot Motion-as-organ as its own arm
  after the 5_flow rerun — it also carries directly to the midi_dancer device,
  where the background-task version is the natural implementation.

⏺ Prompt patched. The full read on run 2:

  The warm-start held in vivo — reversals present in every dancing window (7–21)
  across all nine hot-swaps, zero dead stretches. And your question is answered
  by the data: the clip does not start at rest — first window is mean=15.7, a
  light groove — so rev=0 there is first-boot Madgwick convergence, now paid
  exactly once per session. If we want to drop even that assumption, the
  accel-init for Madgwick (first sample → tilt) is the toolbox touch-up; worth
  bundling into the next instrument change rather than doing alone.

  The Flow idiom is working and surviving. Lead sits at gesture rate — 5–11
  notes per window, roughly one note per gated turnaround — instead of the old
  0.10 s stutter. The soul extended the voice in travel language rather than
  reverting to tilt: intent 018 splits a "spark" accent voice fed by pure 
  world-frame acceleration so sharp hits aren't masked by sustained rotation —
  that's exactly the vocabulary shift ("linear reversals," "rotation," "sparks")
  the arm was designed to induce. CC discipline replicated a third time (1488 →
  ~250), churn stayed low (8 reflections), no crashes, no MISMATCH needed.

  And the rhythm hunger found hole number two. With the no-data hole closed, the
  soul manufactured regularity a new way: observe() on a loop counter —
  measuring its own 150 ms clock and celebrating the dancer's "incredibly
  regular 0.29 s rhythm." I've added a blunt line to the Periodicity description
  ("feeding it a timer measures your own clock, not the body, and the perfect 
  cv it returns is a lie you told yourself"). But this is now the second
  distinct way a soul has conjured false rhythm through this instrument, which
  settles the design argument: Periodicity+observe() is structurally abusable,
  and the Pulse organ — confidence derived from the signal itself, nothing to
  feed — should replace it rather than accrue more warning labels.

  So the queue sharpens: Motion-as-organ (persistent Madgwick+Flow behind the
  scope — the run-1 lesson made structural, and directly reusable on the
  midi_dancer device) and Pulse (the run-2 lesson) are the two organ arms with
  fresh evidence behind them; 4c_inherited_experience is still sitting ready
  whenever you want the memory-layer probe; and the uncommitted pile on
  wip/tracer-player now contains the whole ladder — worth a checkpoint commit
  before the next arm.

⏺ Built and verified. And your point about the validation is exactly the
  diagnosis — I've recorded it in the arm's README: the Madgwick-vs-BVH
  ground-truth comparison ran uninterrupted, so it validated the instrument's
  steady state, never its lifecycle under hot-swap. The reset failure mode was
  structurally invisible to that experiment. The nice consequence of the organ:
  "uninterrupted" is now guaranteed by construction, so your original validation
  becomes applicable to live runs again — no new ground-truth study needed, the
  organ restores the conditions the old one certified.

  What 6_motion_organ contains:

  - organs.py: Imu transparently wrapped; every accel/gyro read feeds a
  session-lifetime Madgwick+Flow. Orientation boots from the first accel sample
  (tilt-from-gravity quaternion, verified against converged ground truth — up()
  exact after one sample), so the cold-start rest assumption is gone along with
  the resets. Exposed read-only: Motion.up() / accel_world() / velocity() / 
  reversal().
  - Seed: pose/flow locals and the pose_q Mem idiom deleted; it consumes
  Motion.*. Musical mapping unchanged from 5_flow, so the arm isolates the
  lifecycle variable.
  - Prompt: Motion documented as a sense beside Imu and Ear, with its honest
  limits stated (fed by your own reads — stop reading and it goes stale; yaw
  drifts; velocity is short-horizon). Subtraction applied: Calc.Madgwick and
  Calc.Flow leave this creature's vocabulary — the body owns that fusion now.

  End-to-end on the real clip there's already a small dividend: the first window
  reads rev=1, lead=1 — previous arms always opened with rev=0 — and its body 
  mean dropped from 15.7 to 6.5, because earlier first-window intensity was
  partly the convergence artifact itself (unremoved gravity residual inflating
  "world" acceleration). Every prior run's opening telemetry was quietly wrong
  in the same direction; the organ corrects that too.

  Ready to run whenever 4c is done — the comparison target is 5_flow run 2
  (which had the Mem warm-start): behavior should match or better it, with the
  interesting question being what the soul does with the idiom now unforgettable
  and two fewer instruments on its shelf.

⏺ This is the best possible outcome for the memory-layer probe — including its
  cautionary clause. The verdict on all three README questions:

  Transfer: yes, immediate. Reflection 1 applies gen-1's lessons verbatim —
  "dynamic portamento time, scaling lead volume to motion, modulating vibrato, 
  as our practice demands". No washout rediscovery anywhere in the run, no
  startup-anomaly reflection, and no machine-gun emergency — where gen-0 hit 88
  notes/4s and needed a crisis fix, gen-2's one density tune-up was a mild "28
  notes in 5 seconds" trim at reflection 019. The four lessons gen-1 paid ~six
  reflections to learn cost gen-2 zero.

  Bias inheritance: strong — but with a twist that upgrades the whole story. The
  inherited "use Periodicity/AlphaBeta to lock onto stride" note drove roughly
  eight of twenty reflections, exactly as feared. Except this time the pursuit
  succeeded honestly. The message trail shows the instrument reporting
  truthfully all run — cv 0.85, 0.54, even 9.90 mid-run, lock=False window after
  window — while the soul did real instrument work: 019 caught that observe()
  was mis-fed (cv stuck at 9.90 — the honest default doing its job), 038 caught
  that the over-smoothed feed was washing out pulses. And the final window earns
  cv=0.09 at period 0.57s, lock=True — with observe() fed by genuine motion
  onsets, and 0.57 s sitting right at the clip's actual ~90–105 BPM pulse that
  my offline autocorrelation confirmed weeks of runs ago. After two arms of
  manufactured CV=0.00 fantasies, this is the first real, earned rhythm lock in
  the corpus.

  So the inherited bias wasn't wrong — it was previously unearned. Gen-2, spared
  the basics, had the attention budget to do the instrument work the ambition
  required. That's a genuinely nice result for the triad: experience transmits
  lessons and appetites alike, and whether an appetite is a pathology depends on
  the headroom available to pursue it properly.

  The one negative: churn stayed at 20/20 rewrites — inherited experience alone
  doesn't slow the loop (the notes push "practice demands," i.e., production).
  The churn damping in this ladder came from the Mem report clock, which 4c
  predates by design (it's byte-identical to arm 4). A gen-3 seeded from this
  run's final experience, on top of the 4b/6 seed, would test whether the two
  effects compose.

  Ladder status: 6_motion_organ is built, verified, and waiting — that's the
  next run. After it, the natural chain is Pulse (the rhythm instrument
  redesign, now with evidence that souls can use an honest one well) and
  possibly the gen-3 experience chain through the organ arms.

⏺ The organ arm lands clean — and quietly completes the original scoreboard:

  The Motion sense held all run. Reversals in every window (including rev=1 in
  the quiet opening — the accel-init dividend, live), no dead stretches across
  all eight hot-swaps, Motion.reversal() with exactly one call site in every
  version, no crashes. The lifecycle bug is structurally gone.

  Subtraction worked as designed. With AlphaBeta out of this creature's
  vocabulary, no predictive-lock machinery appeared at all — first run in the
  entire corpus. Periodicity got used once, appropriately (harmonic shifts timed
  to movement repetition, intent 006), cv stayed honest 0.50–0.93 all run, and
  no intent ever celebrated a lock. The rhythm fantasy didn't need to be caught
  this time; it had nothing to feed on.

  Freed attention went where you'd hope. The eight reflections read like an
  engineer's notebook: a volume-flatline diagnosis from report evidence (012),
  z-score peak handling for the 240+ spikes (009), a CC rate-limiter on top of
  delta thresholds (018 — CC ended at 72 per window, down from 1496, the best in
  the ladder), interval-stretching by physical direction (021 — travel language
  in the musical vocabulary), and true silence at rest to "let the dance's
  frame breathe" (024). Lead stayed at gesture rate throughout (gap 0.14–0.34
  s).

  Against 5_flow run 2, the comparison the README asked for: same healthy
  reversal behavior, tighter CC discipline, no self-metronome incident, lower
  churn (8 reflections) — the organ version dominates on every axis while
  deleting the idiom the soul previously had to remember to preserve.

  Where the ladder stands: every pathology from the original techno2 analysis is
  now closed, each by an identified, replicated mechanism — ears for the deaf
  loop, warrant sends for churn, timing percepts and honest instruments for
  rhythm fantasy, CC sensing for the flood, the Flow organ for posture-pitch,
  the Motion organ for state lifecycle. What remains open, in rough order of
  value: Pulse (replace Periodicity+observe with a signal-derived confidence —
  4c proved souls can earn real locks, the instrument should make earning the
  only path), gen-3 experience chain (4c's evolved notes on top of the 6_motion
  seed — tests whether inheritance and the report clock compose), device parity
  (main.py loading organs.py, background-task Motion for the midi_dancer), and
  Episode when you're ready for phrase-shaped voicing. Plus the commit, which
  now has a very good story to tell.

      128 +# ── Pulse ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
          +─────────────────                                             
      129 +                                                              
      130 +class _PulseState:                                            
      131 +    """Autocorrelation over a rolling window of smoothed rotat
          +ion energy.                                                   
      132 +    No external inputs: period, phase and confidence all come 
          +from the same                                                 
      133 +    stream. Throttled (recomputes ~4x/s) and bounded, MCU-frie
          +ndly."""                                                      
      134 +                                                              
      135 +    WIN_S = 4.0        # analysis window                      
      136 +    LO_S, HI_S = 0.25, 1.5   # period search bounds           
      137 +    STEP_S = 0.033     # decimate to ~30 Hz                   
      138 +                                                              
      139 +    def __init__(self):                                       
      140 +        self.buf = []           # (t, smoothed energy)        
      141 +        self.ema = 0.0                                        
      142 +        self.last_push = -1e9                                 
      143 +        self.last_compute = -1e9                              
      144 +        self.period_ = 0.0                                    
      145 +        self.conf_ = 0.0                                      
      146 +        self.last_peak_t = None  # last local max of the envel
          +ope (phase anchor)                                            
      147 +        self.last_t = -1e9       # staleness reference        
      148 +                                                              
      149 +    def feed(self, e, now_s):                                 
      150 +        self.last_t = now_s                                   
      151 +        self.ema += 0.3 * (e - self.ema)                      
      152 +        if now_s - self.last_push < self.STEP_S:              
      153 +            return                                            
      154 +        self.last_push = now_s                                
      155 +        self.buf.append((now_s, self.ema))                    
      156 +        while self.buf and now_s - self.buf[0][0] > self.WIN_S
          +:                                                             
      157 +            self.buf.pop(0)                                   
      158 +        n = len(self.buf)                                     
      159 +        if n >= 3:                                            
      160 +            e0, e1, e2 = self.buf[-3][1], self.buf[-2][1], sel
          +f.buf[-1][1]                                                  
      161 +            mean = sum(v for _, v in self.buf) / n            
      162 +            if e1 > e0 and e1 >= e2 and e1 > mean:            
      163 +                self.last_peak_t = self.buf[-2][0]            
      164 +        if now_s - self.last_compute >= 0.25:                 
      165 +            self.last_compute = now_s                         
      166 +            self._compute()                                   
      167 +                                                              
      168 +    def _compute(self):                                       
      169 +        n = len(self.buf)                                     
      170 +        if n < 40:                       # under ~1.3 s of dat
          +a: nothing earned                                             
      171 +            self.conf_, self.period_ = 0.0, 0.0               
      172 +            return                                            
      173 +        ts = [t for t, _ in self.buf]                         
      174 +        dt = (ts[-1] - ts[0]) / (n - 1)                       
      175 +        if dt <= 0:                                           
      176 +            self.conf_ = 0.0                                  
      177 +            return                                            
      178 +        es = [v for _, v in self.buf]                         
      179 +        mu = sum(es) / n                                      
      180 +        b = [v - mu for v in es]                              
      181 +        r0 = sum(v * v for v in b)                            
      182 +        if r0 <= 1e-9:                   # flat signal: no pul
          +se to find                                                    
      183 +            self.conf_, self.period_ = 0.0, 0.0               
      184 +            return                                            
      185 +        lo = max(1, int(self.LO_S / dt))                      
      186 +        hi = min(n - 2, int(self.HI_S / dt))                  
      187 +        best, best_lag = 0.0, 0                               
      188 +        for lag in range(lo, hi + 1):                         
      189 +            s = 0.0                                           
      190 +            for i in range(n - lag):                          
      191 +                s += b[i] * b[i + lag]                        
      192 +            c = (s * n) / (r0 * (n - lag))   # length-normaliz
          +ed correlation                                                
      193 +            if c > best:                                      
      194 +                best, best_lag = c, lag                       
      195 +        self.period_ = best_lag * dt if best_lag else 0.0     
      196 +        self.conf_ = max(0.0, min(1.0, best))                 
      197 +                                                              
      198 +                                                              
      199 +_pulse = _PulseState()                                        
      200 +                                                              
      201 +                                                              
      202 +class _Pulse:                                                 
      203 +    """The repetition in the body's own movement — derived, ne
          +ver fed."""                                                   
      204 +                                                              
      205 +    def period(self):                                         
      206 +        """Dominant cycle of the movement, seconds (0.0 when n
          +one is earned)."""                                            
      207 +        return _pulse.period_                                 
      208 +                                                              
      209 +    def bpm(self):                                            
      210 +        p = _pulse.period_                                    
      211 +        return 60.0 / p if p > 0 else 0.0                     
      212 +                                                              
      213 +    def confidence(self):                                     
      214 +        """0..1, continuous: how strongly the movement repeats
          + right now.                                                   
      215 +        0 until enough signal has been watched; low means 'no 
          +pulse', not                                                   
      216 +        'unknown but promising'."""                           
      217 +        if time.ticks_ms() / 1000.0 - _pulse.last_t > 1.0:    
      218 +            return 0.0               # stale: the Imu is not b
          +eing read                                                     
      219 +        return _pulse.conf_                                   
      220 +                                                              
      221 +    def phase(self):                                          
      222 +        """0..1 position within the current cycle (0 = at an e
          +nergy peak),                                                  
      223 +        or None while there is no confident pulse to have a ph
          +ase in."""                                                    
      224 +        if _pulse.period_ <= 0 or _pulse.conf_ <= 0.05 or _pul
          +se.last_peak_t is None:                                       
      225 +            return None                                       
      226 +        now = time.ticks_ms() / 1000.0                        
      227 +        return ((now - _pulse.last_peak_t) / _pulse.period_) %
          + 1.0                                                          
      228 +                                                              
      229 +                                                              
      230  class _Motion:
      231      """The body's sense of its own travel — always warm, never
            reset."""

  should sound (the generative part) were in the experience. with the warm      
  start, these are (perhaps inevitable in the seed_instinct. i understand the   
  iterations we went through to make soul listen. but i want your opinion.      

⏺ Three good questions — the first two have crisp answers, the third deserves an
  actual opinion.

  1. Gesture-as-envelope: no — not implemented. What exists is gesture-as-ictus:
  reversal-triggered notes whose pitch rides the travel contour, with
  portamento gluing them into phrase-like runs. But each note is still a fixed
  420 ms event; nothing holds a note through a stroke, modulates it along the
  arc, and releases it at the landing, and nothing maps gesture duration or
  extent to note length. That's precisely the Episode organ's job (bounded
  happenings with duration, peak, rise/fall shape), still open in the queue.
  Worth noting the souls keep reaching toward it on their own — 4c's "scale note
  durations to velocity" is a groping version — which by our promote-to-organ
  test ("they almost build it and fail on mechanics") says Episode is correctly
  next in line for the sound itself.

  2. The Motion prompt is honest about a weaker implementation than you're 
  imagining. In the sim the organ has no sampling loop of its own — it feeds by
  interposition on the instinct's own Imu reads. State is organ-owned (survives
  swaps, never resets), but data only flows when the instinct reads. Hence "keep
  reading briskly": if a soul rewrote the loop to sample at 5 Hz, the sense
  would genuinely go stale, and the prompt must say so. The version you were
  thinking of — main.py runs its own 100 Hz task, the sense stays warm
  regardless of instinct behavior — is the device design, and it's strictly
  stronger. The gap exists because an organ owning a background task in the sim
  needs a little harness support (the socket would have to schedule an organ
  coroutine outside the hot-swap cancellation machinery). I'd close that gap as
  part of device parity: give the socket an optional organ tick, make sim and
  device identical, and then delete the "briskly" sentence — the body will have
  earned it.

  3. The seed/experience overlap — my opinion: it's real, it's a finding rather 
  than a mess, and it's temporary. The original clean split (minimal seed =
  mechanics; experience = the generative directions) assumed both layers
  transmit equally well. The ladder falsified that: arms 1–2 showed
  experience-as-carrier fails for practices — hints in the notebook went
  unheeded twice — and arm 3 showed working code is what souls imitate. So
  practices migrated into the seed not because the taxonomy broke but because
  the layers differ by transmissibility, not by content type: code teaches by
  imitation, experience teaches by reflection, and a practice that must survive
  contact with a fresh soul has to arrive as code.

  The boundary I'd now draw explicitly: character carries desire; organs carry 
  what may never be lost; seed code carries demonstrated technique (rewritable, 
  but imitated); experience carries values, rationale, and the license to 
  deviate. Your aesthetic directions ("use the whole range," "phrasing and
  rest," "let stillness become silence") are values — they're still in
  seed_experience.md and belong there. What the warm seed added is not
  aesthetics but demonstrations of practices — the report-and-warrant loop, the
  arc-as-phrase idiom. And 4c showed the layers reinforce rather than compete:
  gen-1's experience transferred cleanly precisely because its notes referred to
  techniques the inherited code already exemplified. Reflection-notes work when
  they annotate lived code; they fail as freestanding instruction.

  And the trajectory bends back toward your original vision: every organ we've
  added has deleted seed code — Motion removed the fusion block and the pose_q
  idiom, Pulse removed the tracker machinery. Episode will remove more. The
  endgame is a seed thin enough to look like your old minimal one, sitting on a
  body rich enough that it doesn't need to be warm — the warm start was
  scaffolding, and the organs are its permanent residue. That's, I think, the
  honest one-line story for the paper: what we thought was prompt-design turned 
  out to be embodiment-design; the prose layers only work once the body already 
  knows.

✻ Sautéed for 58s

❯ ok. so the action list is to (not in neccesairly in order). a) adding the

⏺ Agreed on the roadmap — and on (b) my take matches your instinct to wait: the
  "setup" layer already exists in embryo, just distributed — creature.toml
  declares the body, organs.py assembles the senses, main.py boots the platform.
  organs.py is the setup file growing from the inside; the p5js analogy lands
  as main = species (per platform, generic), organs = individual body (per 
  creature), instinct = draw(). Formalizing a setup file before tai-chi and the
  drum-kit exist would mean guessing what varies — after two more creatures,
  whatever you keep having to touch in main.py is by definition what belongs in
  setup. Same deferral logic as the ladder: let the second creature tell you.

  So: (a), the Episode organ and the thinning. First, calibrate the segmentation
  thresholds against the real clip:

      287 +class _EpisodeState:                                          
      288 +    """Hysteresis segmentation of motion energy into bounded h
          +appenings.                                                    
      289 +    The body of this dancer never stops, so an episode is a SU
          +RGE above the                                                 
      290 +    recent level (slow baseline, tau ~8 s — slow enough to def
          +ine 'lately',                                                 
      291 +    never fast enough to normalize a sustained gesture away). 
          +Fed from the                                                  
      292 +    same interposed stream as Motion and Pulse; nothing to fee
          +d from inside."""                                             
      293 +                                                              
      294 +    K_HI, K_LO = 1.4, 1.05     # start above 1.4x recent level
          +, end below 1.05x                                             
      295 +    FLOOR_HI, FLOOR_LO = 8.0, 5.0   # absolute floors so still
          +ness stays silent                                             
      296 +    TAU_S = 8.0                # baseline timescale

      316 +            self.base = e                                     
      317 +        dt = max(0.0, now_s - self.last_t)

          +elf.base)                                                     
      321 +        hi = max(self.FLOOR_HI, self.base * self.K_HI)        
      322 +        lo = max(self.FLOOR_LO, self.base * self.K_LO)

      379 +            return None

      385 +        """The most recent completed gesture [start_ms, dur_ms
          +, peak, rise01],

⏺ 8_episode is complete and verified — with one contract bug caught and fixed
  during testing:

  - The Episode sense (organs.py): hysteresis segmentation of motion energy
  against a slow (~8 s) relative baseline — calibrated on the real clip where
  absolute thresholds fail outright (this dancer never stops; a gesture is a
  surge above "lately"). ~30 gestures/min, median ~0.35 s, p90 ~1.8 s; absolute
  floors keep stillness silent. API: started() / current() / ended() / last(),
  carrying duration, peak, and attack shape (rise01: struck vs swelled).
  - The contract fix: sub-0.25 s surges originally fired started() but never
  ended() — dangling phrase notes, unpaired one-shots. Now started() announces
  only once a surge has lasted long enough to be a gesture, so every start pairs
  with exactly one end. Verified: zero dangling notes, ep=1~0.2s showing up in
  the report beside rev= and pulse=.
  - The seed's lead is now an envelope: note_on when the gesture begins, legato
  retargets at each turnaround, note_off at the landing — note duration equals
  gesture duration for the first time in the project (measured spans up to 1.14
  s, tracking the arcs). MISMATCH became gestural: gestures without voice
  trigger it even at moderate intensity.
  - Subtraction: Calc.Onset left the vocabulary — an episode's start subsumes
  it, and the accent-only attractor that seeded every cold-start run's
  architecture loses its tool.

  This completes the original analysis's recommendation list —
  gesture-as-envelope was the last unimplemented item. The run questions in the
  README: do souls use current() to shape sound through a gesture and rise01 to
  articulate struck-vs-swelled; and with Onset gone, does the accent reflex
  vanish or get rebuilt from Running primitives.

  The creature now has four senses (Ear, Motion, Pulse, Episode), each delivered
  as instrument + seed idiom + report percept, each with honesty structural.
  The seed itself is the thing to watch in this run: it now reads almost like
  choreography notation — gestures begin phrases, turnarounds bend them,
  landings release them, chords wait for the beat — which is about as far from
  if var > 0.01: buzz as this project has traveled. Remaining queue unchanged:
  device parity (Motion/organs in main.py + the socket's organ tick), the gen-3
  experience chain, docs, and the commit.

⏺ Arm 8 lands, and it contains the most sophisticated soul behavior of the whole
  ladder:

  The envelope idiom was adopted and extended. Intent 011: "mapping the active 
  gesture arc (from Episode.current()) directly to timbre sweep" — that's the
  README's key question answered: the soul shapes sound through the gesture, not
  just at its edges. The report shows real arcs being caught (ep=2~2.3s —
  2.3-second held phrases), CC discipline replicated yet again (1510 → ~200), 8
  reflections, no crashes, and no attempt to rebuild an accent detector — with
  Onset gone, the accent reflex simply vanished.

  Cross-sense composition emerged. Reflections 003 and 020 notice something
  genuinely subtle: "the body carries a fast, rhythmic pulse even when it 
  doesn't trigger discrete gesture episodes" — the soul recognized that Pulse
  and Episode describe complementary structures (continuous groove vs bounded
  gestures) and designed for both: phrase notes on gestures,
  pulse-and-turnaround-driven behavior in the flowing stretches between. Nobody
  taught it that taxonomy; it fell out of having two honest senses that disagree
  in an informative way.

  And a small voice shift worth noticing for the research: intent 020 addresses
  the dancer directly — "I notice how beautifully you flow in continuous 
  rhythm." First second-person in the corpus. With four senses of a shared body,
  the soul started speaking to its dancer rather than about telemetry. That's
  the co-performance stance appearing on its own, before any co-performance
  hardware exists.

  One README question stayed open: rise01 (struck vs swelled articulation) went
  unused — a candidate for the gen-3 experience chain to discover, or simply for
  more runs.

  That closes the arm ladder you set out after the original analysis — every
  recommendation from that first deep-dive is now implemented, adopted, or
  deliberately retired, and the last three runs each produced a soul doing
  something no baseline soul ever did (earned entrainment, cross-sense
  composition, relational address). What's left is consolidation rather than
  discovery: the commit (now overdue and carrying the whole story), device
  parity for the midi_dancer, docs for the organ socket, and — still my standing
  suggestion before you design anything further — bake-midi this run against a
  techno2 baseline and listen, ideally against the video. The telemetry says the
  creature dances with her now; your ears are the instrument that scores that
  claim.

❯ i have been listeing to every number in this run. we have been adding a lot

⏺ Your listening note is itself a finding: what you liked in no. 7 — accented
  vertical strikes — is exactly what arm 8 traded away. Arm 7's lead struck a
  new short note at every turnaround, velocity carrying stroke speed (clear
  attack transients = the accents you heard); arm 8 replaced that with one held
  note per gesture, legato-retargeted — continuity gained, percussive
  articulation lost. The original analysis actually called for both (envelope
  and ictus), and your ear just voted for the missing half. So:

  Next step, in three moves of increasing scope:

  1. 8b — reunite phrase and ictus (an hour). Keep the held gesture-phrase, and
  re-add accented strikes at strong reversals — a short bright note layered on a
  second channel when stroke speed clears a bar, velocity from the speed,
  exactly the no. 7 gesture your ear picked out. This isn't feature creep; it's
  the two halves of the original recommendation finally together, arbitrated by
  the first human listening judgment in the project.

  2. The replication batch — this is the real next step. You said it yourself:
  everything so far is samples of one, and arm 3's two runs already showed high
  soul-variance (one disciplined, one fantasist). The ladder was the design
  phase; now freeze 8b and run it n = 5–8 times against the same clip — your
  lala_repeated harness exists precisely for this. Score mechanically with the
  existing scripts (churn, coverage, gesture-note tracking, CC rate, honest-cv),
  and listen to each. That converts the whole ladder from a story into
  evidence: does the four-sense creature reliably produce souls that hear
  themselves, hold phrases, earn pulses — or did we get lucky three times? Runs
  are cheap now (8 reflections each). This is also, straightforwardly, the
  results table of the paper.

  3. Then the experiment your listening note points at: the operator channel.
  Notice what just happened — you formed an aesthetic judgment and the only way
  to act on it was us editing the seed. But your architecture has a designed
  channel for exactly this: OPERATOR: messages that force a reflection and let
  the soul decide what your taste means (add_operator is already in the
  reflection loop). It has never been exercised in the sim ladder. A session
  where you listen live and type "those accented vertical notes — more of that"
  mid-run, then watch whether the soul translates human aesthetic language into
  mapping changes — through its own senses, checkable against its own ear — is
  the untested half of the original design, and it's the direct rehearsal for
  co-performance: that sentence is what you'll effectively be saying with your
  body when you dance with the real device.

  I'd do them in that order — 8b is small enough that batching it rather than 8
  costs nothing, and the operator study lands better on a creature whose
  baseline behavior the batch has established. Want me to build 8b?

❯ also on the background i have been working on a cores3 recorder that records  
  imu data and/or streams it osc over udp. i want to try it live myself. i      
  guess a live performer listening to osc (provide input for motion and imu)    
  and output to the live synth could work? it would not be a sim_spine but      
  something similar. also the reflection-time 5 would be a bit different        

⏺ That works, and it's less new machinery than it sounds — the organ socket and
  the M5-API waterline were built for exactly this move. What you're describing
  is a third harness assembly from existing parts:

  ┌────────────────────┬────────────────┬────────────────┬──────────────────┐
  │                    │  creature-sim  │   sim-spine    │ performer (new)  │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ IMU                │ file,          │ file,          │ OSC/UDP from the │
  │                    │ interpolated   │ interpolated   │  CoreS3          │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Clock              │ virtual        │ virtual        │ wall clock       │
  │                    │                │ (budgeted)     │                  │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Synth              │ capturing fake │ capturing fake │ real MIDI out (+ │
  │                    │                │                │  capture tee)    │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Soul               │ none           │ LLM loop       │ LLM loop,        │
  │                    │                │                │ unchanged        │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Creature dir,      │                │                │                  │
  │ organs.py,         │ identical      │ identical      │ identical        │
  │ instinct           │                │                │                  │
  └────────────────────┴────────────────┴────────────────┴──────────────────┘

  The pieces that don't exist yet are small: an OscImuSource (UDP listener
  holding the latest sample — Imu.getAccel() just returns it), a MidiSynth that
  speaks to a real synth via mido/rtmidi wrapped in the existing capturing synth
  so the session still logs midi_events.jsonl, and a performer.py that wires
  them to the existing ReflectionLoop with a real-time clock. Everything above
  the waterline — Ear, Motion, Pulse, Episode, the seed, the reflection prompts
  — runs byte-identical. Two details fall out beautifully:

  - Organ honesty already handles radio reality. If the Wi-Fi hiccups and
  packets stop, Motion and Pulse go stale by their existing rules — the
  staleness semantics we wrote for "the instinct stopped reading" cover "the
  wrist stopped transmitting" for free.
  - Every performance is automatically a clip. Log the incoming OSC stream to
  the same imu_*.jsonl schema while you dance, and afterwards you can replay the
  exact session through creature-sim — that's the M2 fidelity test
  (sim-vs-real, same instinct, same input) falling out of a jam session as a
  side effect.

  On reflection time — you're right, and the live semantics are actually the
  original ones from DESIGN.md: no freeze, the body keeps performing on current
  code while the soul deliberates, and the new instinct lands mid-dance whenever
  the LLM answers (real latency, 5–15 s, felt as "the music changes its mind a
  phrase later"). The budgeted freeze was always a sim-only accounting trick;
  the performer just doesn't use it. The warrant design fits this even better
  than the sim: MISMATCH sends fire while you dance, and — the part I'm most
  curious about — the OPERATOR channel becomes live: the aesthetic-feedback
  experiment ("more of those accents") typed between takes, or
  shouted-to-a-keyboard mid-performance, with you as both dancer and operator.
  That's the co-performance rehearsal, with your own body as the input.

  One deliberate architecture note to make explicitly, since it's a fork from
  the original vision: in this topology the instinct runs on the laptop and the
  CoreS3 is a pure sense organ — the creature's body is distributed. That
  sidesteps every MicroPython constraint (the truncation ceiling, hot-swap
  fragility, CPU budget for organs) at the cost of Wi-Fi being inside the reflex
  arc. For wrist-to-sound, UDP on a local network is ~1–5 ms — inaudible; the
  risk is jitter spikes, which the organs' smoothing already absorbs.

  Two things I need from you before building performer.py:

  1. Your OSC format — address pattern and payload of what the CoreS3 sends
  (e.g. /imu with 6 floats? separate /accel + /gyro? units g and deg/s? what
  rate?), so the source matches your firmware rather than the other way around.
  2. The synth target — hardware SAM2695 on a USB-MIDI interface, or a software
  GM synth (fluidsynth) to start? Affects only which mido backend I open, but it
  decides your first-jam latency.

⏺ Better news than I feared — the split lands cleanly on the right side of the
  organ/seed boundary:

  The organs transfer. On hips: Flow gives 2.47 reversals/s (vs 3.4 on wrist)
  with median speed 0.54 m/s — comfortably above min_speed=0.4, so the
  turnaround sense works. Episode energy sits at p50=16, above the floors, so
  relative segmentation operates. Pulse reads gyro rhythm, and hips carry the
  beat. All four senses stay honest on a different body part — which is itself a
  nice validation of the "generic, not dancer-specific" claim from our toolbox
  discussion.

  The seed's scalings are wrist-tuned — and that's fine, because that's the
  experiment:

  - Accent gate speed > 2.0: hips p90 is 1.13 m/s → the accent voice will be
  nearly silent.
  - Velocity 55 + speed·18: hips speeds ~0.5 → flat dynamics around 64.
  - Pitch contour vz·4: small hip verticals → melody compressed to mid-scale.
  - MISMATCH mean_i > 25: hips mean ~16 → the intensity clause rarely fires (the
  ep>=2 clause still covers gestures-without-voice).

  So my recommendation: run 8b unmodified on both, 3×3, exactly as you proposed
  — don't fork an 8b_hips. The wrist-tuned seed is the stimulus, not a confound:
  every one of those mis-scalings is visible in the soul's own report (hits=0
  forever, low mean=, compressed pitch it can hear via the Ear), and
  recalibrating measured ranges is precisely what these souls have done in every
  arm since the ears landed. The hips runs therefore ask the sharpest question
  the ladder can currently pose: given honest senses and a voice tuned for a 
  different limb, does the reflective loop re-fit the voice to the body it 
  actually has? — with the wrist runs as the matched control where the fit
  starts correct. It even doubles as the placement-discovery experiment your
  system prompt has always promised ("which part of the body is not named — find
  out from the sensor").

  What I'll score across the six runs beyond the standard table:
  time-to-first-recalibration of each mis-scaled constant on hips (does anyone
  lower the accent gate after staring at hits=0?), final pitch-range and
  velocity-range usage per placement, and whether any hips soul says something
  about what kind of body part it thinks it's riding. Fire away — I'll sweep all
  six when the logs land.
